## Round 20 closure

**RN-32 (ineffective Array.isArray mutation test)**: tests/r3.0d-hardened-intrinsics.test.js
Group 10's second test changed from an Array subclass (`new SubArr()`) to a plain array
(`[1, 2, 3]`).
  - plain array: proto === Array.prototype → audit enters the
    `_apply(_ArrayIsArray, _Array, [v])` branch
  - Array subclass: proto !== _ArrayPrototype → audit rejects in the else branch BEFORE the
    `_apply(_ArrayIsArray, ...)` call → hostile never reachable

The new test rebinds Array.isArray to return false. With captured ref the audit accepts the
plain array (true); with ambient regression the audit returns false. Assertion
`r.result === true` is the catch-point.

Mutation-test verification:
  Regressing `_apply(_ArrayIsArray, _Array, [v])` (inside deepOriginalShapeAudit, anchored
  by `// plain array — additional check: Array.isArray must agree` comment) to ambient
  `Array.isArray(v)` → suite EXIT 1 (2 deepOriginalShapeAudit-specific Array.isArray
  assertions caught).

ALL 30 of HI's exported wrappers continue to have independent mutation-protected coverage,
PLUS the deepOriginalShapeAudit-internal Array.isArray branch is now effectively covered.

     1	/**
     2	 * contracts/r3.0d/hardened-intrinsics.js — R3.0D Hardened Intrinsics Toolkit.
     3	 *
     4	 * Single source of truth for closure-captured pristine intrinsic references and the narrow set
     5	 * of safe wrappers that the rest of R3.0D's contract / runtime path is permitted to use.
     6	 *
     7	 * Codex D2 Round 15 architectural convergence (per SKYLINE directive 2026-06-29):
     8	 *
     9	 *   "只要內部不再執行ambient lookup ... 而只使用module-init捕獲、closure-private的pristine
    10	 *    references，攻擊者替換ambient method後自我還原也沒有意義，因為hostile replacement根本不會
    11	 *    被呼叫。"
    12	 *
    13	 * Rounds 1-15 demonstrated that any defence-in-depth that lets validation code call an AMBIENT
    14	 * prototype method (`arr.push`, `Object.keys`, `String(x)`, `Number.isInteger`, …) is defeated by
    15	 * a hostile replacement that self-restores on its own invocation. The integrity guard then sees
    16	 * a clean state at the next checkpoint, but the validator already used the rebound binding.
    17	 *
    18	 * This module is the FOUNDATION layer. Every other R3.0D contract + the renderer must call ONLY
    19	 * the safe wrappers exported here. Direct ambient property access (`Array.isArray`, `obj.length`,
    20	 * `JSON.stringify`, `Object.freeze`, etc.) is FORBIDDEN throughout the validation path.
    21	 *
    22	 * The wrappers themselves capture pristine intrinsic references at module-init time (BEFORE any
    23	 * caller code can run) and invoke them via captured `Reflect.apply`. They NEVER:
    24	 *   - Use `.call`, `.apply`, or `.bind` on captured methods (those go through
    25	 *     Function.prototype.* which is rebindable).
    26	 *   - Use ambient property lookup on the captured object (which would fire prototype-chain
    27	 *     accessors installed after import).
    28	 *   - Fall back to JSON-round-trip for cloning (which invokes hostile toJSON / drops Symbols
    29	 *     non-deterministically).
    30	 *   - Invoke caller toJSON / Proxy traps / accessor descriptors (`.value` reads on a
    31	 *     `_ObjectGetOwnPropertyDescriptor` result avoid `[[Get]]` semantics).
    32	 *
    33	 * Inputs that carry an accessor descriptor, a Symbol-keyed own property, a non-enumerable own
    34	 * property, a non-Object.prototype prototype, an Array subclass, a mutated Array prototype, or
    35	 * a class instance fail-closed at the structural audit (`deepOriginalShapeAudit`).
    36	 *
    37	 * UMD: Node require / Electron renderer global (R3_0D_HardenedIntrinsics).
    38	 */

    41

    43	  // Every primitive method we may directly or indirectly depend on. References are taken at

    45	  // in IIFE-local variables and NEVER exposed to the api object.
    46

    93

   100	  var _StringPrototypeSlice = String.prototype.slice;
   101	  var _StringPrototypeNormalize = String.prototype.normalize;
   102	  var _StringPrototypeToLowerCase = String.prototype.toLowerCase;
   103

   119	
   120	  // TextEncoder / structuredClone (modern-runtime guaranteed in our target environments).
   121	  // Codex D2 Round 16 RN-28 closure: capture TextEncoder.prototype.encode at module-init so
   122	  // safeUtf8ByteLength never invokes the instance's `.encode` method via ambient property
   123	  // lookup. A hostile TextEncoder.prototype.encode replacement is then unreachable.
   124	  var _TextEncoderCtor = (typeof TextEncoder !== 'undefined') ? TextEncoder : null;

   128	  // Sentinel for failed structural reads (distinguishable from any legitimate value).
   129	  var _SENTINEL = _ObjectFreeze({ __hardenedIntrinsicsSentinel__: true });
   130	
   131	  // ─── PHASE 2 — safe wrappers (the ONLY public surface) ───────────────────────
   132	  // Every wrapper uses captured refs + captured Reflect.apply. None invoke any ambient
   133	  // method, .call, .apply, .bind, JSON fallback, toJSON, or getter/accessor.
   134	
   135	  // _apply(fn, thisArg, argsArray) — invoke a captured function via captured Reflect.apply.
   136	  // Reflect.apply uses the function's [[Call]] internal slot directly, bypassing
   137	  // Function.prototype.call (which is rebindable).
   138	  function _apply(fn, thisArg, argsArray) {
   139	    if (_ReflectApply === null) {
   140	      // Modern runtimes (Node 17+, browsers ~2022+) guarantee Reflect.apply. Fail-closed
   141	      // rather than fall back to .call which is itself rebindable.
   142	      return _SENTINEL;
   143	    }
   144	    try { return _ReflectApply(fn, thisArg, argsArray); }
   145	    catch (e) { return _SENTINEL; }
   146	  }
   147	
   148	  // safeOwnKeys(obj) — Reflect.ownKeys via captured Reflect.apply. Returns an array of
   149	  // string and symbol keys, or null on failure.
   150	  function safeOwnKeys(obj) {
   151	    if (obj === null || obj === undefined) return null;
   152	    if (typeof obj !== 'object' && typeof obj !== 'function') return null;
   153	    var r = _apply(_ReflectOwnKeys, _Reflect, [obj]);
   154	    if (r === _SENTINEL) return null;
   155	    return r;
   156	  }
   157	
   158	  // safeKeys(obj) — Object.keys (enumerable string keys only).
   159	  function safeKeys(obj) {
   160	    if (obj === null || obj === undefined) return null;
   161	    if (typeof obj !== 'object') return null;
   162	    var r = _apply(_ObjectKeys, _Object, [obj]);
   163	    if (r === _SENTINEL) return null;
   164	    return r;
   165	  }
   166	
   167	  // safeGetOwnDescriptor(obj, key) — structural descriptor read (no [[Get]] invocation,
   168	  // so a Proxy's get trap / a value accessor does NOT fire). Returns the descriptor record
   169	  // or null. Note that returning an accessor descriptor (no `value` field) signals
   170	  // tampering / accessor presence — callers MUST check `'value' in d`.
   171	  function safeGetOwnDescriptor(obj, key) {
   172	    if (obj === null || obj === undefined) return null;
   173	    var r = _apply(_ObjectGetOwnPropertyDescriptor, _Object, [obj, key]);
   174	    if (r === _SENTINEL) return null;
   175	    return r;
   176	  }
   177	
   178	  // safeGetOwnPropertyNames(obj) — Object.getOwnPropertyNames via captured ref. Returns ALL
   179	  // own string keys (enumerable AND non-enumerable), or null on failure.
   180	  function safeGetOwnPropertyNames(obj) {
   181	    if (obj === null || obj === undefined) return null;
   182	    if (typeof obj !== 'object') return null;
   183	    var r = _apply(_ObjectGetOwnPropertyNames, _Object, [obj]);
   184	    if (r === _SENTINEL) return null;
   185	    return r;
   186	  }
   187	
   188	  // safeGetPrototypeOf(obj) — Object.getPrototypeOf via captured ref. Returns the prototype
   189	  // (for a Proxy this invokes the getPrototypeOf trap, which is the spec behaviour — callers
   190	  // must handle Proxy-lying via deepOriginalShapeAudit at the boundary).
   191	  function safeGetPrototypeOf(obj) {
   192	    if (obj === null || obj === undefined) return null;
   193	    var r = _apply(_ObjectGetPrototypeOf, _Object, [obj]);
   194	    if (r === _SENTINEL) return null;
   195	    return r;
   196	  }
   197	
   198	  // safeDefineDataProperty(obj, key, value) — install an own data descriptor via
   199	  // [[DefineOwnProperty]] (bypasses [[Set]] / setters on Object.prototype or Array.prototype).
   200	  // Returns true on success, false on failure.
   201	  function safeDefineDataProperty(obj, key, value) {
   202	    if (obj === null || obj === undefined) return false;
   203	    var r = _apply(_ObjectDefineProperty, _Object, [obj, key, { value: value, writable: true, enumerable: true, configurable: true }]);
   204	    return r !== _SENTINEL;
   205	  }
   206	
   207	  // safeHasOwn(obj, key) — own-property check via descriptor read (avoids
   208	  // Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call rebinding AND Function.prototype.call rebinding).
   209	  function safeHasOwn(obj, key) {
   210	    var d = safeGetOwnDescriptor(obj, key);
   211	    return d !== null && d !== undefined;
   212	  }
   213	
   214	  // safeArrayPush(arr, value) — install an own data descriptor at arr.length. Bypasses
   215	  // Array.prototype.push rebinding AND any Array.prototype["N"] setter installation.
   216	  function safeArrayPush(arr, value) {
   217	    if (arr === null || arr === undefined) return false;
   218	    var len;
   219	    try { len = arr.length; } catch (e) { return false; }
   220	    if (typeof len !== 'number') return false;
   221	    return safeDefineDataProperty(arr, len, value);
   222	  }
   223	
   224	  // safeArrayIndexOf(arr, target) — direct equality search via own-indexed reads.
   225	  // Returns -1 on failure or absence.
   226	  function safeArrayIndexOf(arr, target) {
   227	    if (arr === null || arr === undefined) return -1;
   228	    var len;
   229	    try { len = arr.length; } catch (e) { return -1; }
   230	    if (typeof len !== 'number') return -1;
   231	    len = len | 0;
   232	    for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
   233	      var v;
   234	      try { v = arr[i]; } catch (e) { return -1; }
   235	      if (v === target) return i;
   236	    }
   237	    return -1;
   238	  }
   239	
   240	  // safeArrayForEach(arr, fn) — direct-loop forEach. fn is a closure provided by the
   241	  // CONTRACT module (trusted), not the caller. Returns undefined.
   242	  function safeArrayForEach(arr, fn) {
   243	    if (arr === null || arr === undefined) return;
   244	    var len;
   245	    try { len = arr.length; } catch (e) { return; }
   246	    if (typeof len !== 'number') return;
   247	    len = len | 0;
   248	    for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
   249	      try { fn(arr[i], i); } catch (e) { /* fn is trusted but defensive */ }
   250	    }
   251	  }
   252	
   253	  // safeArrayMap(arr, fn) — direct-loop map. fn is a trusted closure. Returns a new array
   254	  // with elements written via defineProperty (bypasses prototype setters).
   255	  function safeArrayMap(arr, fn) {
   256	    var out = [];
   257	    if (arr === null || arr === undefined) return out;
   258	    var len;
   259	    try { len = arr.length; } catch (e) { return out; }
   260	    if (typeof len !== 'number') return out;
   261	    len = len | 0;
   262	    for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
   263	      var mapped;
   264	      try { mapped = fn(arr[i], i); } catch (e) { continue; }
   265	      safeDefineDataProperty(out, i, mapped);
   266	    }
   267	    return out;
   268	  }
   269	
   270	  // safeArraySlice(arr) — direct-loop copy via defineProperty.
   271	  function safeArraySlice(arr) {
   272	    var out = [];
   273	    if (arr === null || arr === undefined) return out;
   274	    var len;
   275	    try { len = arr.length; } catch (e) { return out; }
   276	    if (typeof len !== 'number') return out;
   277	    len = len | 0;
   278	    for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) safeDefineDataProperty(out, i, arr[i]);
   279	    return out;
   280	  }
/**
 * tests/r3.0d-hardened-intrinsics.test.js — adversarial direct-test of every HI.* wrapper.
 *
 * Codex D2 Round 16 RN-31 closure: Section AA in tests/r3.0d-evidence-graph.test.js asserts
 * `hostileInvocationCount === 0` but most assertions pass because the renderer's Step 0 entry
 * intrinsic guard BLOCKs the build before any wrapper is reached. That short-circuit masks the
 * architectural claim — we have not directly proven that the WRAPPER itself bypasses the ambient
 * method.
 *
 * This suite exercises each HI.* wrapper DIRECTLY (no renderer, no entry guard). For every
 * wrapper we install a hostile self-restoring replacement of an ambient intrinsic, invoke the
 * wrapper, and assert TWO independent properties:
 *
 *   1. hostileInvocationCount === 0 — the wrapper did not call the rebound ambient method.
 *   2. wrapper output is correct — the wrapper produced the same result as if no rebind
 *      had happened (i.e. it used the closure-captured pristine reference).
 *
 * If BOTH hold for every wrapper, the architectural claim ("the validation path never invokes
 * ambient intrinsics") is independently verified.
 *
 * Node CLI: `node tests/r3.0d-hardened-intrinsics.test.js`, exit 1 on any failure.
 */
'use strict';

const HI = require('../contracts/r3.0d/hardened-intrinsics.js');

// Capture our own pristine intrinsic refs at THIS module's init (before any test rebind).
// These are used by the runRebind harness to install + restore the hostile binding without
// depending on the (potentially-rebound) ambient Object.defineProperty.
const _origObjectDefineProperty = Object.defineProperty;
const _origReflectApply = Reflect.apply;

let pass = 0, fail = 0;
function chk(name, cond, detail) {
  if (cond) pass++;
  else {
    fail++;
    console.log('  ✗ ' + name + (detail !== undefined ? '  ' + (typeof detail === 'string' ? detail : JSON.stringify(detail)) : ''));
  }
}

// runRebind(container, key, hostileImpl, action)
//   container: the object to rebind on (e.g. Array.prototype, Object, Reflect)
//   key:       the property name to rebind
//   hostileImpl: function to install (its body counts hits + may self-restore)
//   action:    () => result — the wrapper invocation to test under the rebind
// Returns: { result, hits } where hits is the count of hostile invocations.
//
// IMPORTANT: install + restore use the captured _origObjectDefineProperty via captured
// _origReflectApply so the harness itself never invokes the (potentially-rebound) ambient
// Object.defineProperty. Otherwise rebinding Object.defineProperty would cause the harness
// to invoke its own hostile during install/restore, corrupting subsequent tests.
function runRebind(container, key, hostileImpl, action) {
  const orig = container[key];
  let hits = 0;
  const hostile = function () {
    hits++;
    return hostileImpl.apply(this, arguments);
  };
  let result;
  try {
    _origReflectApply(_origObjectDefineProperty, Object, [container, key, { value: hostile, writable: true, enumerable: true, configurable: true }]);
    result = action();
  } finally {
    try { _origReflectApply(_origObjectDefineProperty, Object, [container, key, { value: orig, writable: true, enumerable: true, configurable: true }]); }
    catch (e) { container[key] = orig; }
  }
  return { result: result, hits: hits };
}

// ── Group 1 — Array.prototype methods ────────────────────────────────────────────
(function group1_arrayPrototype() {
  // safeArrayPush — must use _ObjectDefineProperty, not .push
  (function () {
    const arr = [];
    const r = runRebind(Array.prototype, 'push', function () { return 0; }, function () {
      HI.safeArrayPush(arr, 'value');
      return arr.length === 1 && arr[0] === 'value';
    });
    chk('safeArrayPush: hostile Array.prototype.push never invoked', r.hits === 0);

   444	  // deepOriginalShapeAudit(v, depth) — recursive structural validator. Returns true ONLY if v
   445	  // is composed exclusively of plain objects (Object.prototype proto), plain arrays
   446	  // (Array.prototype proto, NOT subclass), strings, finite numbers, booleans, null. Rejects:
   447	  //   - class instances (non-Object.prototype proto)
   448	  //   - Array subclasses (non-Array.prototype proto)
   449	  //   - Proxies that lie about their prototype (lying still produces a wrong proto here)
   450	  //   - Symbol-keyed own properties at any depth
   451	  //   - Non-enumerable own properties at any depth
   452	  //   - Accessor descriptors at any depth
   453	  //   - Function values
   454	  //   - Infinity / NaN numbers
   455	  //   - Recursion past depth cap (32)
   456	  // Calls captured intrinsics only; the audit itself never invokes a [[Get]] on the input
   457	  // (uses descriptor reads exclusively).
   458	  function deepOriginalShapeAudit(v, depth) {
   459	    if (depth === undefined) depth = 0;
   460	    if (depth > 32) return false;
   461	    if (v === null) return true;
   462	    var t = typeof v;
   463	    if (t === 'string' || t === 'boolean') return true;
   464	    if (t === 'number') return safeNumberIsFinite(v);
   465	    if (t === 'function' || t === 'symbol' || t === 'undefined') return false;
   466	    if (t !== 'object') return false;
   467	    // prototype check
   468	    var proto = safeGetPrototypeOf(v);
   469	    var isPlainArray = false;
   470	    if (proto === null) {
   471	      // Object.create(null) — accept; treated as plain object with null proto
   472	    } else if (proto === _ObjectPrototype) {
   473	      // plain object
   474	    } else if (proto === _ArrayPrototype) {
   475	      // plain array — additional check: Array.isArray must agree
   476	      var isArr = _apply(_ArrayIsArray, _Array, [v]);
   477	      if (isArr !== true) return false;
   478	      isPlainArray = true;
   479	    } else {
   480	      return false; // class instance / Array subclass / mutated prototype
   481	    }
   482	    // structural key audit (own keys, including non-enumerable, including Symbol)
   483	    var allKeys = safeOwnKeys(v);
   484	    if (allKeys === null) return false;
   485	    var enumKeysOnly = safeKeys(v);
   486	    if (enumKeysOnly === null) return false;
   487	    // For plain Array: an extra non-enumerable own property "length" is intrinsic (every
   488	    // Array has it by spec). Accept exactly that one extra. Any OTHER non-enumerable or
   489	    // Symbol key is a rejection signal.
   490	    var allowedExtra = isPlainArray ? 1 : 0;
   491	    if (allKeys.length !== enumKeysOnly.length + allowedExtra) return false;
   492	    var len = allKeys.length;
   493	    for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
   494	      var k = allKeys[i];
   495	      if (typeof k === 'symbol') return false;
   496	      var d = safeGetOwnDescriptor(v, k);
   497	      if (d === null || d === undefined) return false;
   498	      if (!('value' in d)) return false; // accessor descriptor
   499	      // Allow the intrinsic Array length (non-enumerable). For all other keys, must be enumerable.
   500	      if (d.enumerable !== true) {
   501	        if (!(isPlainArray && k === 'length')) return false;
   502	      }
   503	      // Recurse into nested
   504	      var child = d.value;
   505	      if (typeof child === 'object' && child !== null) {
   506	        if (!deepOriginalShapeAudit(child, depth + 1)) return false;
   507	      }
   508	      else if (typeof child === 'number') {
   509	        if (!safeNumberIsFinite(child)) return false;
   510	      }
   511	      else if (typeof child === 'function' || typeof child === 'symbol' || typeof child === 'undefined') {
   512	        return false;
   513	      }
   514	    }
   515	    return true;
   516	  }

   518	  // safeObjectCreateNull() — return a fresh object with a NULL prototype using the captured

   520	  // evidence-graph builder which uses `_ObjectCreate(null)` as a Map-shaped sentinel (proto-null
   521	  // bag where attacker-controlled nodeIds like 'constructor' / '__proto__' cannot collide with

   524	  // not accept a custom prototype because every R3.0D caller wants the null-proto case.
   525	  function safeObjectCreateNull() {

   528	    return r;

   531	  // safeObjectAssign(target, source) — shallow-copy `source`'s own enumerable string-keyed
   532	  // properties into `target` using captured intrinsics ONLY. NEVER invokes Object.assign
   533	  // (which routes through ambient [[Get]] / Proxy traps and Object.prototype setters). Each
   534	  // copied key goes through safeDefineDataProperty so prototype setters are bypassed. Values
   535	  // are read via descriptor access (no [[Get]] trap fires). Returns target.
   536	  //
   537	  // Added 2026-06-29 for evidence-node-contract.js per-node sanitized-output construction
   538	  // (replaces `Object.freeze(Object.assign({}, n.observation.params))`). Single-purpose
   539	  // narrow helper: callers that need a more elaborate merge / multi-source assign should
   540	  // compose this with safeKeys themselves rather than expand the helper surface here.
   541	  function safeObjectAssign(target, source) {
   542	    if (target === null || target === undefined) return target;
   543	    if (source === null || source === undefined) return target;
   544	    if (typeof source !== 'object') return target;
   545	    var keys = safeKeys(source);
   546	    if (keys === null) return target;
   547	    var len = keys.length;
   548	    for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
   549	      var k = keys[i];
   550	      var d = safeGetOwnDescriptor(source, k);
   551	      if (d === null || d === undefined) continue;

   553	      safeDefineDataProperty(target, k, d.value);
   554	    }
   555	    return target;
   556	  }

   558	  // deepFreeze(v) — recursive Object.freeze via captured _ObjectFreeze. Skips primitives,
   559	  // freezes both arrays and plain objects + their reachable subtree. Returns v.
   560	  function deepFreeze(v) {
   561	    if (v === null || typeof v !== 'object') return v;

   563	    if (keys !== null) {
   564	      var len = keys.length;

   575	  // invocation anywhere. Walks via safeKeys + manual recursion + direct primitive encoding.
   576	  // Used for canonical bytes (graphId / dedup) AND envelope byte cap measurement.
   577	  function stableStringify(value) {
   578	    if (value === null) return 'null';
   579	    var t = typeof value;
   580	    if (t === 'string') {
   581	      // Captured _JSONStringify on a PRIMITIVE string never invokes toJSON (per ES
   582	      // §25.5.2.2 SerializeJSONProperty: toJSON is only consulted when Type(value) is Object).

   590	    }
   591	    if (t === 'boolean') return value ? 'true' : 'false';
   592	    if (t === 'undefined') return 'null';

   594	    if (isArr === true) {
   595	      var result = '[';
   596	      var len;
   597	      try { len = value.length; } catch (e) { return 'null'; }
   598	      if (typeof len !== 'number') return 'null';
   599	      for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
   600	        if (i > 0) result += ',';
   601	        result += stableStringify(value[i]);
   602	      }
   603	      return result + ']';
   604	    }
   605	    if (t === 'object') {
   606	      var keys = safeKeys(value);
   607	      if (keys === null) return 'null';
   608	      // Manual insertion sort — no .sort dependency.
   609	      var sortLen = keys.length;
   610	      for (var s = 1; s < sortLen; s++) {
   611	        var cur = keys[s];
   612	        var k = s - 1;
   613	        while (k >= 0 && keys[k] > cur) { keys[k + 1] = keys[k]; k--; }

   622	        obj += qkey + ':' + stableStringify(value[key]);

   624	      return obj + '}';
   625	    }
   626	    return 'null';
